gcc -std=c23   -Wall -Wextra -Isrc -Isrc/third_party -Isrc/third_party/cglm/include -Ibuild -MMD -MP -I/usr/include/pipewire-0.3 -I/usr/include/spa-0.2 -fno-strict-aliasing -fno-strict-overflow -D_REENTRANT -I/usr/include/libdecor-0 -I/usr/lib64/libffi/include -I/usr/include/libusb-1.0 -g -c src/third_party/volk/volk.c -o build/third_party/volk/volk.o
g++ -std=c++17                  -Isrc -Isrc/third_party -Isrc/third_party/cglm/include -Ibuild -MMD -MP -I/usr/include/pipewire-0.3 -I/usr/include/spa-0.2 -fno-strict-aliasing -fno-strict-overflow -D_REENTRANT -I/usr/include/libdecor-0 -I/usr/lib64/libffi/include -I/usr/include/libusb-1.0 -c src/vma_impl.cpp -o build/vma_impl.o
g++ build/main.o build/app.o build/third_party/volk/volk.o build/vma_impl.o -o build/vulkanapp -lSDL3 -lvulkan -lm -lpthread -ldl
